# NaIcons - Icons & CupertinoIcons Mapping

This document contains the complete comparison table between Material icons (`Icons`), Cupertino icons (`CupertinoIcons`), and their corresponding unified name in `NaIcons`.

## 📊 Statistics

- **Total Icons Mapped in NaIcons:** `10008`
- **Cross-Platform Icons (Material + Cupertino):** `139`
- **Material-Exclusive Icons (`Icons`):** `8686`
- **Cupertino-Exclusive Icons (`CupertinoIcons`):** `1183`

---

## 🚀 How to Use NaIcons

To render icons seamlessly across platforms, use `NaIcon` with `NaIcons`:

```dart
import 'package:flutter/widgets.dart';
import 'package:nanna_platform/nanna_platform.dart';

// Automatically renders Icons.home on Material (Android/Web/Linux)
// and CupertinoIcons.home on Cupertino (iOS/macOS):
const NaIcon(NaIcons.home)

// With size and custom styling:
const NaIcon(
  NaIcons.settings,
  size: 24.0,
)
```

---
